## 1.3.74 (2026-11-04)
- Back: phone back from the manual opened through a (?) inside Cobros (Préstamos, Cuotas, Me deben summary, loan card) now closes only the manual; Cobros stays open on the same side and tab. Root cause: Cobros had no history entry and caught every back through its `faro-back` handler, even when the back belonged to the manual over it, then re-pushed an entry from popstate (Chrome marks such entries skippable, so Android's back could leave the app). Fix: the provider never hands a back that steps off the manual to the screens under it (phone back and Esc), and Cobros, its add/Abonar forms, Cuotas' add form and Préstamos' forms each own a layer entry pushed in the opening tap. Cobros opened by "Ir allí" still closes with the jump's step.
- Corner button (PAGOS) always opens Cobros on Debo → Cuotas; a stale Préstamos "Ir allí" flag is cleared.
- Cobros arrival from the corner button: grows out of the button (360ms) and its frame pulses once in the side colour (teal / amber in Tron; side accent in day themes). Tapping the button while Cobros is open but off screen scrolls it back with the pulse. Reduced motion: none.
- Me deben | Debo pills: one-shot scan + ring fx on tap and swipe.
- Tron Debo amber calmer: dark Guardar and + with amber ring, neutral chips with faint amber border, dimmer labels, 1px focus with soft glow, amounts #f0b955.
- Tests: bk-cobros.mjs (new, 14 checks incl. every push inside a user tap), cb-fx.mjs (new), bk-back, mn-regress, npm test.

- Cuotas add form: "Sitio o servicio" chips now carry small brand marks and a gear beside the label opens a compact inline editor: rename, reorder (↑/↓), remove (red X), add from the pool (dashed chips), or add your own with an optional icon (shrinkIcon, 64px, ~1KB). Cancelar/Guardar equal width. Chips stay one row that scrolls sideways.

Glyph handles "sub:" names. Saved Cuota gets Line.icon "sub:<id>" or "sub:data:..."; old rows with ↻ whose name matches a preset/pool brand show the mark. Editor: components/faro/sub-presets.tsx. Added weight ≈4.1KB gzip total (marks ~2KB source).

## 1.3.79 (2026-11-04)
- Import sheet: "Cómo descargar tu archivo" chips are editable (gear beside the "?", same inline editor as the Cuotas gears): remove, reorder, re-add Chase/Wells Fargo/PayPal, add your own bank or card with a small icon and free-text download steps (shown when the chip is tapped). - FloatWindow (src/components/faro/shell.tsx) + float.css: 8 resize handles (n/s/e/w + 4 corners). While resizing the frame gets a thin accent ring. .float-win overflow is now visible (handles outside); the bar and body clip their own rounded corners.

EJEMPLO pill beside FARO + thin amber top line while ON. "Reiniciar ejemplo" (with confirm) restores a fresh sample. Export while ON is named Faro_Ejemplo_…; export while OFF holds only his books. Tapping one closes the bubble and goes there (provider goTopic → its own history step; Back returns with the earlier state, e.g. Debo → Préstamos and scroll). Rules in manual-text.tsx `richText(…, ctx)`: no self-link (the topic's own id or title), no link to the screen you are on (places.ts `isHere`, whole screens only), once per paragraph (shared `seen`), and inside forms/import/scan sheets names keep the dotted mark but don't navigate (no typing lost). Registry = links.ts LINKS (149 names) + new LINK_GO overrides (Debo/I owe → Libros → Debo, Préstamos/Loans → Debo → Préstamos, Abonar/Payment → Libros → Me deben). topics.ts: 217 names marked by a one-off audited conversion (ordinary words skipped: "Queda anotado", "Left empty", "Payments that repeat", "PayPal Ahorros", …); 92 topics / 52 bubbles carry links. "Queda"/"Left" left NAME_TERMS (ordinary uses were being bolded). Old accent-coloured `.hint-link` look retired (class kept on the button for tests). Manual → Manual de uso: new tip explaining dotted names. On-hold "Ir allí" change NOT applied.
- Libros ring swipe (touch): Ingresos → Fijos → Variables → Compras → Me deben → Debo → Ahorros → Meta → Retiro → Emergencia → Ingresos, both ways. Pane follows the finger (150·tanh(dx/340), slight fade), commits past 40px when clearly horizontal (|dx| > 1.3|dy|, axis locked after 8px), else springs back; new pane slides in 260ms from the swipe side (`.is-ring`, wrap-around identical); the row's choice lights once (`.ring-lit`; scale side gets its colour tap effect) and the row scrolls into view if off-screen. Guards: inputs, lever rail, charts (non-lucide svg/canvas), [data-noswipe], Cobros, calendar/search; vertical pans untouched (main is pan-y). Each step is a place step like a tab tap (existing convention). Mouse keeps the old per-row swipe. Reduced motion: 160ms crossfade. Fix: a swipe that produced no click no longer swallows the next real tap.
- Central ring swipe (touch): Tengo → Gastaré → Pagos → Meta → Retiro → Emergencia → Tengo (missing pocket skipped), stepping from whichever was picked last; a lens closes the open pocket, a pocket sets the list back to all; lens list / open pocket panel slide in, the lens or tile lights once and scrolls into view. Same engine (lib/faro/ring.ts) and guards. Manual "Deslizar" (ES+EN) describes both rings.
- DebtScale tap effects (user tap or ring landing, never load/Back): bloom from the tap point in the side colour, the dot pulses, pivot + beam settle-wobble (~650ms); then the content below says "here I am" once — Debo: Cuotas and Préstamos tabs, Me deben: header + the two groups — a soft sweep in teal/amber, 80ms stagger (Tron glow, day themes a tint). Reduced motion: colour fade only. DebtScale "?" at right 6px / bottom 6px, aligned with the savings row's "?".
- Debo sub-tabs (Libros only): name larger/tracked + quiet amount under each (Cuotas this month, Préstamos pending), amber hairline indicator that slides between tabs with a faint light-pool (Tron glow), hairline separator, hover/press; "?" stays inline, no wrap at 360r20. The Debo sub-tab now survives leaving Debo (link, Back, swipe).
- Libros → Me deben groups (Sin interés, Con interés), Archivo and Debo → Préstamos loan cards: own design scoped to `.debt-book` (Central Cobros untouched): charcoal panel + the scale's hairline, small teal/amber firefly (Con interés stronger with a ring), quiet count, no silver gloss; Archivo muted with its chevron.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + 14/14; cq-mnA + cq-mnB + bk-back + bk-cobros ALL PASS; dd-flow ALL PASS (D12 reads .dt-name now); new browser-test/t94.mjs 13/13 (dotted link style/keyboard, Balance neto → Me deben → Back to Debo → Préstamos same scroll, no here-link, full Libros ring both ways, short/diagonal/vertical/lever not hijacked); t94c.mjs 4/4 (Central ring both ways, vertical not hijacked). Shots browser-test/t94/ (bubble-balance, manual-deudas, tap-owe mid-animation, debo-tabs, owed-groups; tron/claro 412 + 360r20), dd92/v94-scale-*.
## 1.3.95 (2026-11-04) main 41e2985, source babf59a — No drop when a book arrives; the savings row comes alive
- Bug (Oscar, phone, Tron): swiping Me deben → Debo made the Cuotas | Préstamos tabs and everything under them drop ~6px for a few frames. Cause: the amount fitter (spa/fit.ts) measured amounts with getBoundingClientRect, which includes the slide-in transform; with the new pane shifted 56px right, the Balance neto amount looked like it ran off screen, so its font shrank (line 36 → 30px) until the slide ended and it was refitted. Fix: fit.ts subtracts the sideways translation of every transformed ancestor (`slideX`) before deciding an amount overflows, so amounts are measured where they will rest (also fixes the finger-drag and every other sliding pane).
- Same check on every crossing: savings tabs (Ahorros/Meta/Retiro/Emergencia, tap or swipe) jumped 55–78px because Desk applied the tab's focus (Todas / the pocket ledger) in a useEffect, one painted frame late → now a useLayoutEffect (screens.tsx). The income/fixed/variable books' empty note rose 4px on arrival → inside Libros panes it fades in place (`.swipe-pane .fx-note { animation-name: ring-fade }`).
- Measured frame by frame (412×860, touch, rAF): before — Me deben → Debo tabs 6.5px, Debo → Me deben 4.2px, Compras → Me deben 6.5px, savings tabs 55/77.6px, books 4px; after — 0px for all 20 Libros ring steps (both ways), scale taps, savings taps, and the 12 Central ring steps, Tron and Claro; rows and scrollY 0.
- Savings row alive (Oscar): Ahorros (purple), Meta, Retiro, Emergencia each in their own colour (Tron-ized on Tron): a soft bloom from the tap point (~560ms, inside a clipped `.vb-fx` layer), the icon pulses (`scale`, so Tron's lock-on keeps playing), then the first three blocks below say "here I am" once (sweep + hairline, 80ms stagger, radius taken from the visible card). Only on a tap or a ring landing (`faro-vault-tap`, scoped to the swiped hub), never on load or Back. Selection look untouched (brackets, purple scope). Reduced motion: tint fade (Claro etc.); Tron keeps its rule of no animation. Overlays only, no layout change (verified: positions identical before/after).
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + 14/14; t94 + t94c + dd-flow + bk-back ALL PASS; new browser-test/drop95.mjs (rects per frame through the scale crossings), drop95c.mjs (every ring crossing, max vertical movement of incoming content; CENTRAL=1 for Central), t95.mjs 15/15 (bloom/pulse/glow in the tab's colour, clears, no shift, nothing on Back/load, ring landing, reduced motion). Shots browser-test/t95/ (tron/claro 412: meta-, emergencia-, ahorros-mid).
## 1.3.96 (2026-11-04) main 01a83a2, source c54da21 — Savings row: its own quiet "deposit" animation
- Oscar: 1.3.95's savings tap was too strong and copied Me deben/Debo. The bloom from the tap point, the icon pulse and the "here I am" sweep + hairline glow are gone from the savings row (DebtScale keeps its own). New, subtler, themed on filling up (debts.css "1.3.96 savings row", shell.tsx useVaultHello):
  - Button: the icon lifts 1.5px and settles (`translate`, 420ms, so Tron's lock-on `transform` still plays); a 1.5px line in the tab's colour fills the bottom edge left → right (inset 16%, scaleX 0→1 in ~350ms, then fades; 620ms total). Day/dark themes lean the tone 30% toward the text colour so it reads on a pale pill; Tron sits 4px up (clear of the lit frame) with a white-hot head and a small glow.
  - Content: the pocket's first card gets one faint diagonal sheen (115°, narrow band, 500ms, opacity .15 Tron / .08 other themes, no outline, no glow); the next one or two blocks get a very faint light rising from their bottom edge (to ~40% height, .12 Tron / .07 others), 70ms stagger. Classes `.is-vsheen` / `.is-vrise` (old `.is-vhello`, `.vb-bloom`, `data-pulse` removed); button attribute `data-rise`.
  - Unchanged: tab colours, selection, brackets; only on a tap or a ring landing, never on load or Back. Reduced motion: line + blocks fade in place (no rise) on Claro etc.; Tron no animation.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; t95.mjs rewritten for 1.3.96 15/15+M2 ALL PASS (fill/rise/sheen/rise-fill in the tab colour, sheen on the first block with no box-shadow, 70ms delays, no old bloom/hello, clears, no shift, nothing on Back/load, ring landing, reduced motion Claro + Tron); drop95c Tron + Claro WORST 0px; t94, t94c, dd-flow, bk-back ALL PASS. Shots (animation frozen at fill 330ms / icon 160ms / blocks 260ms, browser-test/s96.mjs): browser-test/t95/{tron,claro}-{meta,emergencia}-96-mid.jpg.

## 1.3.97 (2026-11-04) main fb8ca95, source 61f2b99 — No empty frame when a savings tab or debt side is tapped
- Oscar (Android, Tron): after tapping Ahorros/Meta/Retiro/Emergencia the whole area under the row blinked empty for a split second. Cause: the tapped tab mounts a brand-new pane (React key change) and `.swipe-pane.is-vault` played `vault-in` (opacity 0 → 1, 360ms). While the new pocket mounted the main thread was busy, so the animation sat at its 0% frame (opacity 0) for 1–3 frames (~0.3 s at 4× CPU throttle) before jumping in. Tron added its own `tron-rez` on every new `.card`/`.ridge` (opacity 0 + clipped away), and Me deben's `.cobro-swap` faded from .65.
- Fix: `.swipe-pane.is-vault { animation: none }` (styles.css). Panes swapped by a tap or ring step get `is-swap` (shell.tsx BooksHub; Desk lens list in screens.tsx) and debts.css turns off the arrival fades inside them: Tron rez on cards, fx-note and cobro-swap fades (the hub's very first pane keeps Tron's rez). Ring landings keep their slide but start at .72 opacity instead of .35; reduced motion uses `ring-soft` (.6 → 1) instead of fading from 0. The 1.3.96 savings lights (icon rise, fill line, sheen, rise-fill) are unchanged.
- Evidence (browser-test/nf97.mjs, per-frame rAF sampling of the content's effective opacity, child count, height, faded/clipped descendants, CPU throttle 4×, Tron + Claro; JSON in browser-test/nf97/frames-{before,after-thr4}.json): before, every savings tap, Me deben tap and Libros/Central landing had 2–59 frames at opacity 0; after, savings + DebtScale taps hold opacity 1 every frame, top-row taps ≥ .65, swipe landings ≥ .72, 0 bad frames. Shot of the first frame after tapping Retiro at 4× throttle: browser-test/nf97/tron-retiro-first-frame.jpg.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; nf97 ALL PASS; t95 ALL PASS (lights unchanged); drop95c Tron + Claro WORST 0px; t94, t94c, dd-flow, bk-back ALL PASS.

## 1.3.98 (2026-11-04) main 8f2909b, source 78100fc — Asesor Desplegar (on by default, asks, resets), Debo sub-tab glide + light, screen entrances restored
- Asesor → Desplegar row (Oscar, Tron phone): gap above/below was 16/30 px (checkbox 26/40) at 360/412/360r20, because the row's generic `.unfold-all { margin: 2px 0 16px }` sat inside the 14px grid gap; tablet masonry (row first, full width) was 24/12 at 820 and 22/12 at 1280. Now `.advice-stack > .unfold-all.is-advice { margin-block: 2px }` → 16/16 (box 26/26) on phones incl. 360r20; masonry (pinch.ts) sets the row's margin-bottom so the space under it equals the space above it (to the tabs or the top bar) → 22/22 at 820, 20/20 at 1280.
- Desplegar starts ticked (every card, part and "more" open; a card with no explicit choice follows Desplegar, so findings that arrive later open too). Unticking asks with Faro's dialog (lighthouse seal inline with the title, Cancelar | Aceptar, equal width): "¿Plegar las tarjetas?" / "Quedan plegadas por ahora. La próxima vez que abras Faro, Desplegar vuelve a estar marcado y se abren todas." (EN "Fold all the cards?" / "They stay folded for now. The next time you open Faro, Unfold is ticked again and they all open up.", Cancel | Accept). Rule chosen: folded is kept in memory only (module variable, never saved) — it survives moving between screens/tabs and Back within this run, and resets to ticked on the next app open/reload. Cancel keeps it ticked. Tapping a tower part no longer unticks Desplegar.
- Hint: when the Asesor opens, the checkbox gives one soft glint 650 ms in (1.15 s; Tron: teal glow + thin ring + brief brightness; day themes: a soft accent-tint ring), and at most once more after 45 s with no touch/scroll/keys while the Asesor is open. box-shadow/filter only (no layout), never loops, nothing under reduced motion.
- Debo → Cuotas | Préstamos (Libros): on a tap only (never load, Back or a name link) the content glides 14 px in from the side of travel and lights in from .88 opacity (220 ms; React reuses the element, so a/b keyframes alternate to restart); the amber bar sends a soft radial light up under the name (~360 ms, rises 5px and fades) on top of its slide. Reduced motion: opacity .88 → 1 and a plain light fade. Me deben in Libros has no sub-tabs (only its two groups), so nothing to add there.
- Screen entrances (Oscar: Ajustes looked buggy): recorded frame by frame (aj98.mjs, Tron/Claro, CPU 4× and 1×) on 1.3.94 (pre-1.3.95, main be0ec89), 1.3.96 and 1.3.97: no code regression — every version rezzed all cards at the same instant with no stagger (Tron) and nothing at all on day themes, and the mount work ate the first 100–500 ms of frames so the rez landed half-done, all at once. The only real regression found: 1.3.97's `.swipe-pane.is-vault :is(.card,.ridge){animation:none}` also removed Tron's rez from a Libros book's first pane. New entrance.ts (`useScreenEntrance` in Shell, keyed on the section): the first visible outermost panels of the screen that opens get `data-enter` + `--enter-i` (sorted top→bottom, max 7 steps); Tron: `tron-rez-in` (starts as a faint ghost of the top 42% at .4, never blank) 40 ms + i·65 ms, with the corner brackets drawing out from the corners and flashing once (`tron-brackets`, background-size); other themes: rise-fade from .45 / 10 px, 30 ms + i·55 ms. Reads then writes (no layout thrash), MutationObserver for 700 ms catches late panels. Never inside `.is-swap`/`.is-ring` (the 1.3.97 tab/swipe no-empty-frame rule stays), never under reduced motion, panels with their own entrance (Asesor cards) keep it.
- Manual: desplegar-asesor (ES+EN) — starts ticked, asks before folding, folded lasts until Faro opens again, glows once on open; [[Asesor]]/[[Advisor]] dotted links; keys advice.unfold.ask/body.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + test:faro 21/21; t94, t94c, t95, nf97, bk-back ALL PASS; drop95c Tron + Claro WORST 0 (CENTRAL=1 mode errors at its own rowY selector on 1.3.96 too — test bit-rot, Central ring covered by t94c/nf97). New: browser-test/m98.mjs (gap probe), t98.mjs 34/34 (gaps equal, default checked, glint once + clears, untick → dialog, inline seal + equal buttons, Cancel keeps, Accept folds, same visit stays folded, reload → ticked + open, no errors, reduced motion; Tron/Claro 412 + Tron 360r20), t98d.mjs (Debo frame check: content opacity ≥ .88 every frame, 0 px vertical (content and tabs), glide 14 → 0 stepped, bar lit, never empty, nothing on Back/load, reduced-motion fade; Tron + Claro), ent98.mjs 13/13 (Ajustes/Central/Libros/Estudio/Asesor × Tron/Claro, stepped timeline: staggered, min visible .17 Tron ghost / .45 day, 0 blank steps, 0 px layout movement; savings swap unmarked; reduced motion none). Shots browser-test/t98/: {tron,claro}-412-row.jpg (glint frozen at peak), tron-360r20-row(-rest).jpg, {tron-412,claro-412,tron-360r20}-dialog.jpg, {tron,claro}-debo-lit.jpg, {tron,claro}-ajustes-enter-260.jpg; frames aj98/.

## 1.3.99 (2026-11-04) a9ad273, source 5948164 — One premium colour menu everywhere (quick set without look-alikes, spectrum + HEX, Mis colores)
- Oscar (Tron phone): the "Ícono y color" → COLOR DE LA BURBUJA grid had near-duplicates (three greys/beiges, two mints, light blues, lilacs) and the trigger showed a plain grey circle. Cause of the look-alikes: the grid drew every swatch through the day-bubble wash (`washForInk`), which squeezed the 24 colours to a min OKLab ΔE of .010 (the greys were 1% apart), and Tron showed those washed pastels instead of its lights.
- New shared `ColorPick` (src/components/faro/color-pick.tsx + color-pick.css, maths in src/lib/faro/palette.ts): used by the bubble/line look (Libros +, bubble editor, pinned looks of Retiro/Primerica and savings lines), savings pockets (Meta/Emergencia/pocket lines), bank accounts, the line form's colour, Ajustes → Estructura category tree (Fijos/Variables), Compras categories (paint and new category). Remesas lines use the bubble look. People (Me deben) have no colour picker (auto hues), unchanged.
- Quick set (15 + Personalizado = 2 rows of 8; one row of 16 on wide docks): #e75e6a #ffa289 #dc7106 #b28d04 #79a304 #8dd28c #52d7c1 #11a6aa #079ed9 #6b89f9 #bab3fe #ac72e3 #e4a3e9 #d562af #494d54, chosen by maximin over raw, washed and Tron ΔE: min ΔE raw .071 (was .064), washed .042 (was .010), Tron .081 (was .057). Removed: #c3bdb3 stone, #89939e grey, #876b59 brown (washed to the same grey as graphite), #edb161 (vs orange/ochre), #c5c463 (vs ochre/olive), #0bac77 (vs mint/green), #4dd1ef and #8dc2fe (light blues vs sky/blue), #fd9cba (vs coral/orchid). PALETTE stays as is, so stored colours, Tron lights and default hashes are untouched; a category with a removed colour keeps it and shows it selected under "Actual" (or "Mis colores").
- Swatches show what the theme draws: day = the washed bubble, solid hosts (categories, Compras) = the colour, Tron = its light. Selected = check mark + ring.
- Personalizado (rainbow chip): saturation/brightness area + hue bar (pointer events: finger, S-Pen, mouse; arrow keys), HEX field (3 or 6 digits), live preview on a bubble and on the trigger/icon chips, Cancelar left / Guardar right. Owns a history entry: Back closes it (a module-level popstate/faro-back guard runs first; Guardar/Cancelar step back silently).
- Mis colores: last 6 saved customs in `ledger.myColors` (syncs, backs up; sample mode writes its own ledger). Ink on any colour: `inkOn()` picks dark or white by WCAG contrast for previews, chips and the trigger.
- Trigger: glossy gem of the chosen colour with the chosen icon (Palette when none), thin spectrum ring; Tron draws it as a Tron bubble.
- Manual: new topic "Color personalizado / Custom colour" (color-propio), links "Color personalizado"/"Custom colour"; icono-color and f-color updated.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test (palette.test.ts: ΔE, dedupe, quick-set gaps, hex parse, contrast adjust, HSV, recents); browser-test/cp99.mjs ALL PASS in Tron and Claro (two hosts, quick, drag + HEX, Back closes spectrum then picker, Mis colores after reload, old #c3bdb3 kept); t94, t95, nf97 (Tron, Claro), bk-back ALL PASS. Shots: browser-test/cp99/*.jpg.
- After publishing (in the tree, ships with the next release; not yet live): (a) second tap on the selected swatch (quick, Mis colores, or the current-colour chip) confirms and closes the colour view back to the "Ícono y color" trigger showing the icon in that colour; first tap still only selects/previews; tapping the spectrum's preview bubble = Guardar + close (`ColorPick onConfirm`; wired in BookLook and the Compras paint; tree / line form / new Compras category have no colour view to close, so there it is a no-op). (b) The current colour no longer gets its own sparse "ACTUAL" row: it sits at the right end of the colour header line ("COLOR DE LA BURBUJA (?) … ● ACTUAL"; the word hides below an 18rem dock, e.g. 360r20, leaving the ringed swatch), a colour already in Mis colores stays there selected (no row jumping), Mis colores chips follow their label directly, and every section label uses the dock's label style (same as ÍCONO). New `head` prop; the line form's colour title moved into it (one-line change in line-form.tsx). Manual (icono-color, color-propio) ES+EN describe the second tap and the preview tap. cp99.mjs P2b/P2c/P8b–P8d added; ALL PASS Tron + Claro; bk-back ALL PASS.

## 1.3.101 (2026-11-05) main 321c02c, source 8e5eecd — Foto o captura: read receipts and subscription screenshots on the device, in a batch, review, save
- Entry: in every spending book's + editor (Cuotas, Compras, Variables, Fijos) a compact "Foto o captura" pill (camera; picks one or many pictures, the system chooser also offers the camera) + an aperture square (capture=environment) sit at the right end of the Concepto head row — no new row. Buttons + hidden inputs inside the label (after its input), sheet portalled to <body>. LineForm got a `scan` prop; screens.tsx passes `<ScanEntry from={draftKind} />` for the four spending kinds.
- OCR on device, reusing the Retiro stack (tesseract.js 7, eng+spa fast, ./ocr/ lazy + SW-cached): new src/lib/faro/scanrun.ts — one worker per batch, images one at a time (≤1280 px wide / 2800 tall, greyscale, dark-mode screenshots inverted), per-image progress, Cancel/Back terminates the worker. Lines come with word boxes (a lone icon letter far right is dropped). Speed (headless, aarch64): 0.4–1.1 s per image once loaded, +1.3–2 s first load; real 1080×1194 Play screenshot ≈ 3–4 s. Never uploaded; thumbnails only in memory.
- Parser src/lib/faro/receiptparse.ts (+ receiptparse.test.ts, 14 tests, in test:faro): Google Play list/detail EN/ES ("Next payment: $59.99 on Jan 28, 2027", "Próximo pago: US$59.99 el 28 ene 2027", "$13.99/mes · Se renueva el 12 nov"), Apple ("Renews November 20 ($10.99)"), confirmation emails ("App:", "Price … per year", "Next billing date"); one picture → one draft per subscription; "Your plan includes"/benefit lines ignored; app name cleaned (" - tagline", ": tagline" dropped), plan → note; cadence from Yearly/Anual/Mensual/"/mes" cues, else inferred (next charge > 62 days → Anual) and flagged amber. Receipts: merchant from the top lines, strongest TOTAL label (Grand/Order Total, Total a pagar…; never Subtotal/Tax/Cash/Change), ES/EN dates (day-first when Spanish), $ or C$; Compras category guess (ropa/tech/hogar/otros), restaurants/cafés → Variables (Entretenimiento·Restaurantes / Hormiga·Café), gas → Variables·Transporte.
- Icons: library brand first (matchBrand); unknown apps get their app tile cropped from the screenshot (longest run of unlike rows/cols right of the block, rounded, shrinkIcon 64 px ≈2 KB) — on by default, removable (×) / restorable (✓); a generic library mark is the last resort.
- Review sheet (receipt-scan.tsx + scan.css): batch progress bars, then one card per draft: include switch, mark, Concepto, delete; $/C$ + amount, day, frecuencia (the editor's FreqPick sheet, with "Otro…" and its remembered custom rhythms); source thumbnail, book chip (Cuotas/Compras/Variables/Fijos), note. Phone widths use 4 rows (frequency next to the book), ≥400 px sheet 3 rows; nothing cut at 360r20. Guessed fields amber until touched. Duplicates (same concept + amount + day already in the ledger) flagged "Ya está en tus libros" and unchecked. Started from Cuotas → every draft to Cuotas; else subscriptions → Cuotas, receipts → the book it was opened from (Compras keeps a café → Variables). Guardar N / Cancelar equal. Nothing saved until Guardar; saved drafts are ordinary lines via upsertLine in one commit ("Escanear N") → Central, calendar, Estudio, Asesor, Excel, search, sync, sample-mode isolation as usual. Back/Escape close the sheet (cancelling a running batch); the frequency sheet is its own Back step.
- Manual: escanear "Foto o captura"/"Photo or screen" (ES+EN) with [[Cuotas]]/[[Plans]] and [[Biblioteca de íconos]]/[[Icon library]] links; link terms "Foto o captura"/"Photo or screen".
- Fixtures (synthetic, tools/fixtures/scan/make.py): play-dark-en.png (3 apps, dark, benefits), play-es-3apps.png (YouTube/Duolingo/Spotify), receipt-publix.png, receipt-en.png. Real OCR accuracy: all 4 fixtures 100% (concept, amount, date, cadence, book, brand); Oscar's real Play screenshot (private, not in the repo): 3/3 drafts, amounts/dates/plans correct, Anual (2 inferred → amber), Google One brand, 2 cropped app icons.

## 1.3.105 (2026-11-05) main 26a350a, source ab74127 — Floating window: smooth resize and move
- Oscar: resizing/moving the floating window (Ventana flotante) stuttered and the edge lagged far behind the finger. FloatWindow (shell.tsx) + float.css only.
- Drag engine: pointer capture, coalesced samples, the newest finger position (plus the browser's own predicted event, ≤12 ms ahead, only while samples are fresh) painted once per frame straight to style; no React state and no class changes until release (drag state is `data-moving`/`data-edge` attributes, so spa/fit.ts's observer no longer re-fits the document mid-stroke). Release lands on the exact last finger position; lostpointercapture ends a drag too.
- Move = compositor-only translate3d. Resize = the frame follows every frame while the content keeps its width (frozen px inside the new .float-clip) and re-flows only on a finger pause (path < 12 px over the last 80 ms, 200 ms wait, skipped when input is pending), on release, or mid-stroke on fast devices (last settle ≤ 24 ms, ≥ 100 ms apart, adaptive). Height follows every frame.
- The window lives in a full-screen `contain: strict` .float-layer, so a frame resize lays out only that layer. The big drag shadow is a separate .float-shade scaled on the compositor (frame keeps only the thin accent ring). Main-thread animations inside the window and non-composited ones behind it pause for the drag and resume after. Content subtree memoized, so committing a drag re-renders only the frame.
- Touch (any-pointer: coarse): every edge grip ≥ 32 px, corners 44–52 px, the extra reach outside the frame.
- Measured, browser-test/fw105.mjs at 4× CPU, Tron Asesor, medians of interleaved runs on a loaded sandbox (lag = edge vs finger, ms; catch-up after lift; long-task ms). Phone 412 (n=3): s 157→130 lag, catch-up 663→280, LT 849→436; e 64→64, catch-up >1400→347, LT 342→286; se 166→162, catch-up >1400→258, LT 1539→317; move 157→143, catch-up 270→86, LT 611→203. Tablet 1970 (se/move n=7, s/e n=3): s 207→193, catch-up 530→463, LT 512→241; e 431→400, catch-up >1400→463, LT 1128→330; se 405→406 (p95 920→880), catch-up >1400→472, LT 1489→448; move 366→347, catch-up 553→519, LT 877→370.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test ALL PASS (incl. test:faro); fw105 CHECK=1 phone + tablet ALL PASS; fl85 ALL PASS; nf97 Tron + Claro ALL PASS; bk-back ALL PASS. Results browser-test/fw105/R-*.json, S-*.json.

## 1.3.106 (2026-11-05) main b8fcc15, source 763aa84 — Central: one active thing among Tengo · Gastaré · Pagos and their corner buttons
- Oscar (Tron phone): with Gastaré picked the card lit and the others dimmed, but the + and the red Pagos button stayed fully bright (same with Pagos picked). Rule now: exactly one thing is active, everything else goes low-profile.
- A picked card (tap or swipe ring) lights itself AND its own corner button; the other two cards and buttons dim. An open corner flow (+ Sumar ingreso, − Anotar gasto, Debo/Me deben → Cobros) lights ONLY that button; all three cards (the one under it too) and the other two buttons dim. Closing the flow (button again, Cancel/Save, phone Back) returns to the card that was picked before. Nothing picked = everything bright (as before).
- screens.tsx Desk: `btnOn` (quick in/out, or Cobros while it holds the focus) / `cardOn`; cards get aria-pressed only when they are the active thing (`is-picked` still follows the lens, so the pick ripple does not replay), corners get `.is-low`; `.lenses[data-focus]`. Picking a card while Cobros stays open moves the focus to the card; the Pagos button then takes it back (and calls the panel back) instead of closing it. A card tapped to leave a +/− flow becomes the active card (no toggle-off). QuickAdd's onClose no longer forces Tengo/Gastaré unless nothing was picked; Desk's back handler leaves an open +/− flow to QuickAdd (back used to also un-pick the card).
- styles.css "1.3.106": low corner = opacity .62 (same as dim cards) + saturate(.7), no glow; active corner keeps its glow; 180 ms opacity/filter/shadow transitions, reduced motion = instant; no layout change. Data and what each button opens unchanged.
- Manual: Tengo (ES+EN) gets one sentence: lo activo brilla y lo demás se atenúa.
- Published from a clean clone of `source` (1.3.105) + only these 4 files (screens.tsx, styles.css, topics.ts, sw.js) via /tmp/pub106.sh; tarjetas 1.3.107 WIP not shipped.
- Tests (on the clean build): tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + test:faro 35/35 (clean tree); new browser-test/t106.mjs ALL PASS (Tron 412, Claro 412, Lino 820): each card → card+button bright, + / − / Pagos button → only it bright, swaps, close/Back restore, card tap from a flow, Cobros + card + button, no layout shift; t94c ALL PASS; nf97 Tron + Claro ALL PASS; drop95c WORST 0; bk-back ALL PASS. ## 1.3.107 (2026-11-05) main 4c5840a, source d0ba1b5 — Tarjetas: credit cards under Debo → Préstamos, updated from a bank screenshot read on the device; the payoff plan covers them
- Oscar: "credit is a form of loan". Libros → Debo → Préstamos (full functions) and Central → Pagos → Debo → Préstamos (1.3.104 charcoal amber group) gain a "Tarjetas" group next to the loans. Top figure of Préstamos = loans + cards; the line under it splits Préstamos / Tarjetas. Group head: dot, Tarjetas, count (hidden under 21rem), card total (amber), aperture (batch screenshot), + Añadir tarjeta, pencil (edit state), (?).
- Model (src/lib/faro/cards.ts): ledger.cards (CreditCard: name, issuer, mark, colour, art, network, last4, currency, limit, due day, APR, promo 0% until, autopay, rewards note, manualOnly, snaps) + ledger.cardsOff. Every update stores a dated reading (CardSnap: balance, available, limit, min, due date, APR, src shot|manual, seen payment ids), never replaced or lost; archived cards keep their history. Limit = typed limit, else the reading's, else balance + available. Utilization, trend (delta vs the previous day's reading), sparkline, "creciendo/bajando" read the readings.
- Presets Chase Freedom Unlimited (manual only: Chase blocks screenshots), Wells Fargo Active Cash, PayPal Cashback World Mastercard: names, marks, colours and art only, no numbers. Virtual until they get data (no load-time write, existing data untouched); delete in edit state with a confirm (remembered in cardsOff, synced, backed up, sample mode has its own ledger), "Empezar de cero" removes all presets in one confirmed step, RotateCcw/Sugeridas restore them (with history). Empty state: "Añade tus tarjetas…" + Añadir tarjeta (works for any issuer and a new user with no presets).
- Card art (card-art.tsx): stylized SVG mini cards (44×28 in rows, 64×41 in sheet headers), not traced logos: Chase dark-blue wave + green accent, Wells Fargo graphite with red diagonal band + gold stripe, PayPal navy with a big light-blue P; VISA text / Mastercard circles. Other cards: wave/band/glow/plain in their colour (ColorPick), auto-matched to the issuer when recognized. Last 4 only from the user's data.
- Rows: art · name (···last4) / util bar · min · due · trend chip (amber up, teal down) · balance (amber). No data → compact "Actualizar" chip. Names never wrap or ellipsize: full name, else short name (no network word), then one shared size (≥82%), then ···last4 steps aside; meta parts step aside (due, min, "desde el", bar) before anything clips.
- Card sheet: art, balance, trend, sparkline, use bar with "baja $X para 30%", limit/available/min/due/APR(+promo)/interest a month (≈ when estimated)/autopay/rewards, "Añade la tasa para un mejor consejo" when no APR, readings + payments history, Editar / Eliminar, Abonar | Actualizar (chooser Cámara / Imágenes / Manual).
- Screenshots (cardparse.ts + 1.3.101 scanrun stack, 1.3.103 chooser): one or many; label/value columns joined by row from word boxes; EN/ES (Current/Outstanding balance, Saldo actual/pendiente, Available credit/Crédito disponible, Credit limit/Límite, Min payment due/Pago mínimo, Payment due date/Fecha de pago, Statement balance, APR/Tasa de interés, Autopay); rewards, cash back, offers ignored; FICO shown as info only, never stored. Matched by last4, issuer, name (asks when unsure, amber). Preview per card: old → new balance, delta, available, limit (derived = amber), minimum, due date, include switch, art; unknown card → "Añadir tarjeta" prefilled; Guardar N applies all in one commit. Pictures never stored or sent.
- Money: card balances count in Debo/Préstamos, DebtScale, net balance, the 6-month debt chart (cards from their readings), payoff plan, Central Pagos, Estudio (Préstamos card), Asesor, Excel ("Tarjetas" sheet with readings and payments), calendar (due dates), search. Abonar = a transfer line (xfer, inactive, cardId) like the bank importer's card payments — purchases were already expenses, so the payment never counts as spending twice; it lowers the balance until the next reading (a reading saved later already includes it).
- Plan de pago "¿Cuál pago primero?": amount per payment + Cada cuánto (the editor's FreqPick, incl. Otro… custom rhythms), saved in debtPlan.cadence (synced). Minimums first, the rest to the target; revolving card interest on what is left; promo 0% months free; avalanche by effective APR; per-payment split, reason line, estimate note (24%) for cards with no APR. Asesor: new "Tarjetas · ¿Cuál pago primero?" card (next card, amount per payment, debt-free date, use, monthly interest cost) + a Credit cards point in "Cómo va mi crédito".
- Manual ES+EN: new "Tarjetas de crédito" and "Actualizar con una captura"; Préstamos and Plan de pago updated; links Tarjetas de crédito / Credit cards, Actualizar con una captura / Update with a screenshot.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + test:faro 46/46 (new cardparse.test.ts 6, cards.test.ts 5: synthetic PayPal-dark, WF-light, ES, columns, two cards, matching; limit derivation, utilization, trend, archive, payment no double count, totals/net/series/plan). Fixtures tools/fixtures/cards/make.py (synthetic look-alikes). Playwright browser-test/t107.mjs A–G ALL PASS Tron 412 (+ C/E/F 360r20, E/F Claro), real OCR on fixtures; bk-cobros, dd-flow, bk-back, t101 A–D, t103 A–C, nf97 Tron + Claro ALL PASS. Private real screenshots (not in the repo): 2/2 matched, every value right (balance, available, derived limit, minimum, FICO info), ≈4.5 s for both.
## 1.3.108 (2026-11-05) main 0e1d589, source a600816 — Central corner buttons switch cleanly; + / − panels say hello in their colour
- Bug (Oscar, screenshot after −): with + or − open, tapping the Pagos corner button closed everything (on his phone it fell back to a card) instead of opening Pagos. Cause: QuickAdd's unmount gives its history entries back with an async `history.go(-n)`; Cobros mounted in the same commit and pushed its layer entry first, so that step-back landed as a phone Back on Cobros and closed it (and left `__faroSkipPop` set). Fix (screens.tsx): Cobros opened from an open flow gets `wait` and takes its history entry only after that popstate (600 ms fallback); the panel shows at once. All six button→button switches open the tapped flow directly with only that button bright (1.3.106 rule), from no card or a picked card.
- Phone Back from + / − no longer picks Tengo/Gastaré when no card was picked ("closing restores the previous card"); QuickAdd's popstate close passes `"back"`. A finished entry still lights its card as before.
- Effects check: Pagos (Cobros) already had its own (grows from its button + frame pulse in amber #ffc24a Tron / #c6ae86 day); kept. + / − panels only had the neutral sheet-swap fade, so they get `quick-hello` (styles.css): a hairline in the panel colour runs out along the top edge from the corner button's x over a soft top wash (teal #3fd6f5 Tron / #2fa58a day for +, orange #ff9d2a / #d08a3c for −), ~0.7 s, once per opening, transform/opacity only on an absolutely placed overlay, never on load or Back; reduced motion = wash fade (Tron's global reduced-motion rule turns it off like everything else). Distinct from the savings "deposit" fill and DebtScale bloom/sweep.
- Optimization: `sheet-swap` starts at opacity .45 and `cobros-arrive` at .4 (were 0): no blank first frames at CPU 4× (1.3.106 had 2–3 per opening).
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + 46/46; new browser-test/t108.mjs (all 12 ordered pairs from none/Tengo + Back + chain + hello colour/fit/no shift/Back + Cobros arrival) ALL PASS Tron, Claro, Claro reduced, Tron reduced; fr108.mjs frames 4× 0 empty; t106, nf97, bk-back ALL PASS; drop95c Tron + Claro WORST 0. Shot browser-test/t108/tron-412-minus-to-pagos.jpg.

## 1.3.109 (2026-11-05) — Card rewards (cash back) count as money he has: read from any screenshot, Me deben → "Recompensas de tarjetas", Canjear, Estudio sources
- Oscar: the Wells Fargo Active Cash screen shows a cash rewards amount next to the balance; that is money he has, it should count in the other bubble, and the sum should say where it comes from for Estudio. Later: detect rewards in ANY screenshot (any wording, any bank), "Current/available" = money, "Year to Date/lifetime" = earned info only; every number in a screenshot is a row he can edit or remove.
- Model (cards.ts, types.ts): CreditCard.rw = dated rewards readings (RewardSnap: amount, points as a note, earned year/lifetime as info, src shot|manual|redeem), synced/backed up with ledger.cards, sample mode isolated. rewardsOf / rewardsAt / rewardsTotals / rewardsEarned / addRewards / redeemRewards / rewardLines. CardSnap also keeps statement, last payment and "Otros datos" notes.
- Where it counts (design call): rewards are money the BANK owes him, so they live on the Me deben side (the "other bubble" of the Debo/Me deben scale), never subtracted from the card debt. netBalance: owed = people + rewards (`lent`, `rewards` parts); DebtScale bubble, Balance neto, the 6-month owed line (from readings) and Asesor's Balance neto include them. Libros → Me deben and Central → Pagos → Me deben: top figure = people + rewards with the split under it ("Personas $X · Recompensas $Y"), and a teal "Recompensas de tarjetas" group (per card: art, name, as-of date, amount; tap → Canjear). Tengo stays this month's income only (a balance is not income until redeemed).
- Canjear (RedeemSheet, from the group or the card sheet): Crédito al saldo = xfer line (inactive, cardId + rwCard) lowers the card balance, rewards reading drops by the same amount: no spending, no income, Balance neto unchanged. Depósito al banco = active income line (rwCard, no cardId) + rewards drop. Partial amounts allowed. Never counted twice (tests).
- Card UI: teal chip "★ Recompensas $X" on the card row (the word steps aside before the amount on narrow rows), rewards block with Canjear + history entries (Recompensas · captura/manual, Canje · crédito/depósito) in the card sheet, "Recompensas ($)" in the manual Actualizar/Añadir form (a rewards-only manual update is allowed); last payment, statement and kept notes in the sheet facts.
- Reader (cardparse.ts): rewards labels EN/ES (rewards, cash rewards, cash back/cashback, reward dollars, recompensas, reembolso(s), bonificación, points/puntos); a $ amount on the label, or values under it side by side ("$a $b" / "Current Year to Date") or stacked with the caption under each value; Current/available/disponible/actual = balance; Year to date/YTD/en el año/earned/lifetime = earned info (span year|life); no caption = first value, amber. Rates ("1,5% en todo") and offers ignored; points = note. A rewards line is never taken as the balance. Last payment read. Every other labelled number becomes an "Otros datos" row (ads flagged and off by default). Rewards-only crops (no card name) are kept: merged with a card already read in the same batch with the same amount, else the card is guessed from the wording ("cash rewards" → Wells Fargo, "cash back" → PayPal, "Ultimate Rewards" → Chase, "Cashback Bonus" → Discover) or the card that already has rewards, painted amber.
- Card preview: every number is an editable row with × to remove (available, limit, minimum, statement, APR %, last payment, due date), rewards row (old → new, switch on by default, ×), "Ganado en el año · info" row (×), Otros datos (switch = keep as note, editable label/value, ×; labels wrap instead of being cut). FICO stays info only.
- General "Foto o captura" scan (receipt-scan.tsx): a rewards line in any picture adds a "Recompensas" row (card matched or best guess in amber, switch on, editable, ×) saved as a rewards reading; the rewards/balance lines of that picture no longer become expense drafts.
- Estudio: new fold "De dónde viene tu dinero" (Con el tiempo, earnings.ts/earnings.tsx): the year's income by concept (top 5 + Otros) with "Recompensas de tarjetas" as its own teal source, per card and a 12-month bar from the readings; rewards count when earned (reading increases, or the bank's year-to-date figure when larger); rewards deposits are left out of the income sources (counted once). The "Lo que me deben" card shows the rewards line.
- Asesor: "Recompensas de tarjetas" point (total, per card, redeem as credit on the highest-APR card); the cards plan card adds one sentence. Excel: Tarjetas sheet gets a Recompensas column; new "Recompensas" sheet (every reading, earned, redeemed, points). Search: rewards per card under Me deben. Manual ES+EN: new "Recompensas de tarjetas" and "De dónde viene tu dinero"; Tarjetas, Actualizar con una captura, Escanear, Me deben, Balance neto updated; links Recompensas de tarjetas / Card rewards, Canjear / Redeem, De dónde viene tu dinero / Where your money comes from.
- Fixtures (synthetic): tools/fixtures/cards/make.py adds paypal-stacked.png (stacked Current / Year to Date) and rewards-crop.png.
- Published from a clean clone of `source` (1.3.108) + only these changes via /tmp/pub109.sh.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + test:faro 53/53 (cardparse 10: rewards EN/ES, stacked, lifetime, points, YTD-only, Otros datos, last payment; cards 8: readings/asset/not subtracted, credit redemption keeps net, deposit = income counted once in Estudio); t107 A–G ALL PASS Tron 412 (+ C/E/F 360r20, E/F Claro); t101 A–D, t103 B ALL PASS; new browser-test/t109.mjs A–D ALL PASS Tron 412, Claro 412, Tron 360r20 (real OCR on fixtures: batch read + merge, edit/remove rows, chips teal, Me deben bubble/group/split/Balance neto, Canjear credit + deposit, Estudio sources, receipt-scan rewards row). Private real screenshots (local only, not in the repo): all matched, balances/available/derived limit/minimum, rewards Current and Year to Date read correctly, rewards-only crop merged (alone: guessed Wells Fargo, amber). Shots browser-test/t109/.

## 1.3.111 (2026-11-05) — Faro for Android (WebView app) + the web bridge it needs; browser/PWA unchanged
- Oscar (20:46): build Faro as an Android app like Weekly/Iris, "con cuidado". App: package com.oscarvelz.faro, "Faro", lighthouse icon from the PWA icon (adaptive, bg #101412), Tron-dark system splash #0E1114 then Faro's own splash; loads https://oscarvelz.github.io/Faro/ live (web releases arrive by themselves), offline through WebView cache + the service worker. Source android/ on `source`; CI .github/workflows/android.yml (source branch only, android/** changes; never main/Pages).
- Signing: new private key (PKCS12, alias faro, RSA 4096, cert SHA-256 8f:e1:ad:7c:41:08:da:ff:7d:03:11:50:10:f8:72:11:98:48:7a:ca:69:46:d9:b9:46:98:f2:b2:92:63:4b:98), never committed. The Hark GitHub app cannot set Actions secrets, so CI builds an unsigned APK artifact, it is signed privately (apksigner v3) and pushed to the orphan branch `android-signed` (Faro.apk + VERSION + NOTES.md), whose workflow publishes release android-N. If secrets FARO_KS_B64/FARO_KS_PASS are ever added, CI signs and releases by itself.
- Web side (all feature-detected on window.FaroNative; no-ops in a browser/PWA): new src/spa/native.ts (startNative from spa/main.tsx). Back: the phone's Back calls FaroBack() → history.back() through Faro's own layers/places; the provider's "nothing to close" branch (provider.tsx, only when FaroNative exists) or no screen mounted (lock/sign-in) or no history step within 400 ms → FaroNative.atRoot("Pulsa atrás otra vez para salir"), second Back within 2 s closes. Steps are counted with an early popstate listener + Navigation API currententrychange (layers stop popstate propagation). Bars: status = meta theme-color (statusbar.ts), navigation = colour at the bottom edge, light/dark icons by luminance. Downloads: blob/data links with download (backup .json.gz, Excel, PDF) are kept (createObjectURL map, revoke deferred 60 s) and written via saveBegin/saveChunk/saveEnd into Downloads/Faro (MediaStore, no storage permission), toast "Guardado en Descargas › Faro: name". Fingerprint: lock.ts uses BiometricPrompt (BIOMETRIC_WEAK | DEVICE_CREDENTIAL: fingerprint/face or the phone's PIN/pattern/password) when FaroNative exists; WebAuthn path unchanged in browsers; marker "native" per device (localStorage, not synced).
- Native: file chooser (capture → camera app via ACTION_IMAGE_CAPTURE + FileProvider, no CAMERA permission needed; images → photo picker, multiple up to 12; other files → GET_CONTENT */* so .json/.gz/.vcf/.csv are never hidden); external links (Manual dotted links, bank sites, mailto/tel/intent) open outside, only oscarvelz.github.io/Faro stays inside (also for window.open); offline first-open page "Faro necesita internet la primera vez"; bridge methods refuse non-Faro pages; in-app update offer from releases android-N (once a day per version, PackageInstaller, "Tus datos y tu cuenta se quedan igual").
- Phase 2: Android share target for images (SEND/SEND_MULTIPLE image/*): copied into the app cache, pulled by the page in chunks; new lazy share-inbox.tsx/.css (loaded only inside the app) asks "N imágenes recibidas · ¿Qué son? Revisas todo antes de guardar." → Tarjeta de crédito (existing CardScan preview) or Recibo o gasto (existing ScanSheet, now exported, from "purchase"); nothing saved without the preview; options stack at 360r20 so nothing is cut.
- Ajustes: "Instalar app Android" row (only on Android browsers, never inside the app) → confirm → https://github.com/Oscarvelz/Faro/releases/latest/download/Faro.apk. Manual: new topic "App de Faro para Android" ES+EN (Cuenta group); links aj-android.
- Storage: the app's WebView storage is separate from Chrome's. Synced: the whole ledger (incl. Mis colores, cards, settings in the ledger) + cloud backups (14 days). Local-only (stay behind): fingerprint lock choice, device auto-backups (IndexedDB 7 days), calculator slips (Instrumentos), finished-plan archive (faro-due-file), Despertar log/dismissals, report drafts, theme/day-night/language device keys, layout prefs (float/split/folds/pins), sample mode.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + test:faro 53/53; new lock-native unit test 2/2; new browser-test/na111.mjs A–D ALL PASS Tron 412 and Claro 360r20 (browser unchanged, Android-Chrome row + confirm + APK URL, mocked FaroNative Back/root/layer/screen, bars, backup download bytes, share → chooser → both previews → Back); bk-back, t106, t107, t109, t110, nf97 ALL PASS on the 1.3.111 build (same as 1.3.110 baseline). Base build of 1.3.110 source was byte-identical to live main. APK: Actions run 1 success, manifest checked (package, label, perms INTERNET/NETWORK_STATE/USE_BIOMETRIC/REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES, no CAMERA), apksigner verify v3 OK. Not testable headless: real WebView, camera, BiometricPrompt, share sheet, offline, install/update on the Galaxy S24 Ultra / Tab S10 Ultra. Self-review only.

## 1.3.112 (2026-11-05) — The bottom nav never blinks or gets covered when switching books
- Bug (Oscar, 21:51, Claro, phone video): in Libros, Fijos → Variables or Compras → Variables hid the bottom nav (Central · Libros · Estudio) for ~0.2–0.5 s and the Variables content (Gastos variables + Personas card and its "Agregar persona" button) showed in its place, then the nav popped back in place. Going to Fijos kept the nav.
- Cause: the dock was not on its own compositor layer (no will-change outside phone landscape). A book swap animates the new pane (`study-in` / `ring-in`, transform + opacity) on the compositor. When the new pane reaches under the dock (Variables with the Personas card; Fijos and Compras end above it), Chrome promotes the dock mid-switch (overlap with an animating layer; also when the page turns scrollable). On the phone that fresh layer showed unrastered until the slide ended and the dock was demoted again, so the pane showed through. Not the hide-on-scroll (scrollY stays 0, no transform, no is-away) and not a z-index or re-mount (same node, z 20, no stacking context above it).
- Fix (styles.css only): `.app-frame .dock { will-change: transform; }` keeps the dock on its own layer at all times, so nothing changes for it at a switch (the hide-on-scroll already moves it with translate3d, so no new cost). Found by the new test: a ring swipe in Libros (pane enters from 56 px; finger drag up to 150 px) counted as sideways page overflow, so the phone viewport widened for those frames (page zoomed out, dock jumped 92 px and grew 44 px). Below 1100 px the main column is the screen's width, so `.app-frame .main-pad { overflow-x: clip; }` hides nothing on screen; clip makes no scroller (sticky bars and vertical scroll unchanged). Animations unchanged, 0 px shift.
- Published from a clean clone of `source` (1.3.111) + only these changes. Base build of that source was byte-identical to live main.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + test:faro 53/53; new browser-test/nb112.mjs (CPU 4×, rAF sampling: dock box unmoved, no transform/away/opacity loss, elementFromPoint at its centre + 4 midline points = the dock, every frame) A all 90 ordered switches between the 10 Libros books, B Libros ring swipes both ways (20), Central ring swipes (12), Estudio swipes (4), C the dock's layer is its own (WillChangeTransform) before/during/after Fijos → Variables: ALL PASS Tron 412, Claro 412, Tron 360r20, Claro 360r20. On the 1.3.111 build B fails (viewport widens on Libros ring swipes) and C fails (layer only from FixedPosition); headless cannot show the unrastered-layer blink itself, so A passes on both. nf97, drop95c Tron + Claro WORST 0, t106 Tron + Claro, t110 Tron 412 + Claro 360r20, na111 Tron 412 + Claro 360r20, bk-back: ALL PASS. Not testable headless: the blink on the Galaxy S24 Ultra itself. Self-review only.

## 1.3.114 (2026-11-06) main 4e3ec02, source 70552b9 — Tablet mode built for the Tab S10 Ultra: nothing edge to edge, both docks open in landscape, the scale in the top bar, comparison and due-date panes; the left drawer is never see-through again
- Oscar (23:48–00:26, Tab S10 Ultra 14.6", CSS ~1850–1970 landscape / ~1155–1200 portrait, S-Pen): rows too long and the wide screen badly used; side docks should use their whole column again; the left drawer sometimes opened fully transparent with cards/text showing through and taps not answering; Ahorros/Meta/Retiro/Emergencia and the books stretched across; the DebtScale had no place; add genuinely useful tablet panes (no duplicates); no card wider than ~55% of the screen. PHONE FROZEN: everything is scoped to >= 1100 px one-pane (media queries or a size check); phone 360–412 shots pixel-identical to 1.3.113.
- Drawer bug, cause: the away dock slid on a `transform` transition (1.3.84), which Chrome runs on the compositor. Starting fully off-screen, that transition can stay pending (no start time from the compositor) while nothing else repaints: data-recall said open, but the dock stayed off-screen or undrawn and the pull tab kept covering it (measured: `getAnimations()` pending, currentTime 0 for 600+ ms, reproducible on 1.3.113). On top, the rail's face had no background at >= 1100 (only the right dock had one), so whenever the left drawer was painted it was see-through and taps between its buttons hit an empty layer. Fix (tablet.css + shell.tsx): opaque face (var(--bg)) and a soft shadow on both drawers; the drawer and its tab move on left/right (main thread, fixed elements, no page reflow); opening is instant (in place on the first frame after the tap), closing slides 0.2 s; setRecall finishes any transition still pending after 260 ms; reduced motion = no transitions.
- Docks: the 1.3.84 centred 104 px tiles are gone; the buttons share the dock's whole height again (both docks). At >= 1600 wide (Tab landscape) both docks stay docked as side panels (no drawer, no pull tab); below (portrait 1155–1200) the away dock is the drawer.
- Top bar: the Me deben · Debo scale (unchanged design, bloom, glow, history) is portalled into the top bar, centred (>= 1100, one pane); below 1100 and in split panes it stays above the books. Me deben / Debo open below the bar, laid out in two balanced columns (lists + Archivo | chart + plan); Balance neto sits beside its label; Cuotas · Préstamos tabs kept together (760 px); Tarjetas first unchanged.
- Layout: book cards flow their rows in columns (min 340 px; amounts beside names); sibling books side by side from 1100 (portrait too); bubble books in a single card use 24 grid columns; savings books: controls left, list right; Central: money strips + Ahorros left (5fr), month board right (7fr); Ajustes masonry takes 3 columns when the board is >= 1500 px (screens.tsx, size check; phones never reach it).
- New tablet panes (book-side.tsx, only mounted on a one-pane screen >= 1100): (1) Contra <mes pasado> beside Fijos, Compras and the four savings books: this month vs last month per line (before → now, change, nuevo / ya no), total and change; the books themselves never show last month. (2) Próximos 30 días beside Debo → Cuotas: cuotas in date order with days left and overdue count; the Cuotas list has no date ordering. (3) S-Pen/mouse hover on rows (any-hover). Estudio and Asesor unchanged (they only lose the 104 px of the now-docked dock at >= 1600).
- Not done: t107 E2 (Tarjetas header tools overflow at Claro 360r20) is a phone fix and the phone is frozen; left for Oscar's go-ahead.
- Published from a clean clone of `source` (1.3.113 + notes 516b595) + only these changes.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + test:faro 53/53; new browser-test/tb114.mjs: A drawers at CPU 4× (plain, rapid 5-tap bursts, tap during a swipe, after frozen/hidden → visible; opaque face, opacity 1, elementFromPoint on 5 points incl. gaps = drawer on the first frame after the tap; gone with pointer-events none on close) PASS Tron 1200×1970 left+right, Tron 1155×1850 right, Claro 1155×1850 left, Claro 1478×924 right; the same test on 1.3.113 FAILS (transparent face, drawer off-screen with data-recall set). B layout (no overflow, rows ≤ 640, savings 2 columns, scale centred in the bar clear of brand/tools, both docks docked at 1970) PASS 1970×1200, 1850×1155, 1200×1970, 1155×1850, 985×1200, 925×1155 (split halves: scale stays inline). C scale (Me deben 2 columns, bloom, bar 0 px shift, Debo tabs, Tarjetas first, Back closes) PASS Tron + Claro. D no framed card/row wider than 55% of the viewport PASS Tron 1970×1200, 1850×1155, 1200×1970, 1155×1850, Claro 1970×1200. Phone frozen: browser-test/ph114.mjs, 8 screens (Central, Libros, Variables, Me deben, Debo, Ahorros, Estudio, Ajustes) pixel-identical (AE 0) to 1.3.113 at Tron 412 and Claro 360r20. nb112 B/C Tron 412, nf97 Tron + Claro, drop95c Tron WORST 0, bk-back, t110 B Tron 412 ALL PASS; fw105 at 1970 comparable to 1.3.113; fl85 harness does not open its float window on 1.3.113 either (not run). Shots browser-test/tb114/. Not testable headless: the real compositor on the Tab S10 Ultra. Self-review only.

## 1.3.119 (2026-11-06) — Bandas: one side band at a time again on tablet/desktop; swipe loops through both bands
- Oscar (06:16): bring back hiding the right band while using the left one, the tap that brings the right one out, and using it hides the left, "tal y como estaba antes". (06:31): swiping goes through every option of both bands as one loop: after the left band's last screen the next swipe opens the right band's first (right band out, left band away at once), from the right band's last back to the left band's first; backwards mirrors it.
- How it was before: up to 1.3.113 (source 3e277dd; shell.tsx homeSide → .is-hide-left / .is-hide-right, styles.css drawer rules) one band was docked, the band of the screen in use, and the other waited behind its pull tab as a drawer; 1.3.114 kept that below 1600 px. 1.3.114 (source 70552b9) added a tablet.css block at (width >= 1600px) and (height >= 600px) that docked both. That block is removed, so alternation is back at every width >= 1100 (portrait and landscape, desktop included). Kept from 1.3.114: opaque drawers + shadow, left/right slide, instant open, 260 ms finish fallback, pointer-events none when closed, full-height dock buttons, DebtScale in the top bar, grids, comparison panes, 55% card limit; the page takes the freed column.
- Swipe loop (shell.tsx): one-pane >= 1100 px, a horizontal swipe (touch, pen or mouse drag) walks DOCK_RING = left band (Central, Ahorros, Meta, Retiro, Emergencia, Cambio, Ajustes) then right band (Ingresos, Fijos, Variables, Compras, Cuotas, Estudio, Asesor), wrapping both ways. The docked band always follows the screen (homeSide); Me deben/Debo count as Cuotas' place. Each step is one history entry, so Back walks the same screens with the matching band. Below 1100 px (phone) and in split panes the old rings are untouched (Central ring, Libros ring, Estudio).
- Files: src/components/faro/shell.tsx, src/components/faro/tablet.css, public/sw.js. Published from a clean clone of `source` (1.3.118, 7bdf977); the patch applied cleanly on top of 1.3.117 Asesor and 1.3.118 Remesas.

## 1.3.120 (2026-11-06) main 64fde09, source 8cf90d8 — Tablet/desktop: the scale is the only thing in the middle of the top bar, on every screen; a tap slides Me deben · Debo out
- Oscar (06:18–06:19, Tab S10 Ultra, photo of the 1.3.114 bar): the wide Me deben · scale · Debo pill crowded the top bar. At rest only the scale (its emblem) shows, a bit larger, with a soft glow that never covers anything and tells with its colours that there is something to see; a tap makes it react and slide Me deben and Debo out to each side with a fine transition and light; the scale must be on every screen, Central included (it was only in Libros). Fully polished, proportional, crisp at every tablet size and DPR 1.6. PHONE FROZEN: nothing changes below 1100 px or in split panes (the scale is only mounted in the top bar at >= 1100 × 600, one pane).
- Emblem (debts.tsx ScaleEmblem): a drawn balance on a 40-unit grid, one stroke weight (1.6; cords 1.15), round caps, column + plinth + base, finial, beam pinned at its centre; both pans hang straight down from the beam ends while the beam leans toward the heavier side (same ±7° lean as before, pans follow by translate, no distortion). Teal pan = Me deben, amber pan = Debo, frame in the theme's ink (Tron: cyan with a faint glow). 34 px inside a 46 px disc (was a 15 px lucide icon in a 28 px pivot).
- Resting glow (top-scale.css): two soft lights (108 px, ±12 px off centre) behind the disc, teal left, amber right, breathe slowly (6.4 s, out of step); a side with amounts glows clearly, an empty side stays soft but visible (day themes too). - Tap: the disc tilts and blooms a little, a ring of light leaves it; the two halves of the original pill (same look per theme, split at the emblem) slide out from behind it with one light sweep each (teal to the left, amber to the right). Second tap, Back (own history entry via useLayer) or a tap anywhere outside folds them back in. Folded halves take no taps and no focus. A transition still pending 140 ms after the tap is finished, and any still running at 720 ms is finished too (the 1.3.114 compositor lesson; a busy main thread once left them half-way), so the halves are always where the state says.
- Halves keep their job: in Libros they open Me deben / Debo under the bar exactly as before (bloom, dots, "?", Debo Cuotas · Préstamos, Tarjetas first); on any other screen a half takes you to that side in Libros (one history step; Back returns). ## 1.3.121 (2026-11-06) — Home-screen widgets for the Android app: "Faro · Mes" (phone) and "Faro · Panel" (tablet); web: the bridge that feeds them
- Oscar (06:23): a professional, beautiful, very useful widget with the app's most important information on the phone's home screen; a different one with a much better dashboard for the tablet (Galaxy S24 Ultra, Galaxy Tab S10 Ultra, One UI).
- Design brief. Phone, "one number, one picture, one action": Queda (Tengo − Gastaré, Central's own figure) is the number; the month bar is the picture (the whole bar is Tengo in teal, the orange part is Gastaré, the teal left over is Queda), with Tengo · Gastaré · Usado under it; + (teal) and − (orange) are the actions. At 4×2 three quiet columns follow, split by hairlines, no boxes: Próximo pago (first due from today: name, days left in amber within 3 days, amount), Me deben · Debo (the scale as one bar, teal against amber) and Ahorro (the month's savings against its goal as a purple ring). 4×1: Queda, the next payment and −. 4×3+: plus the next three dues. Tablet panel (6×4 and up): column 1 the month (Queda, bar, Tengo/Gastaré/Usado, Pagos with its share of income in the debt signal's colour) and six months of Tengo vs Gastaré as paired bars on one scale; column 2 spending by category (Estudio's slices, top five, orange; savings purple; a neutral grey bar under each = last month) and Ahorros (ring, total, this month vs goal, the pockets); column 3 what is due in 14 days (overdue red, ≤3 days amber, later grey, total), Me deben · Debo with Neto, and the Tasa (C$, source and date, US$100 in córdobas). Smaller panels drop parts instead of squeezing them (5×3: no categories; ≤ 4×3: no chart, no savings).
- Taps (every part): the number → Central; + / − → Central with the + or − list open (the same tap a finger makes); next payment / a cuota → Central → Pagos; a card due → Libros → Debo → Préstamos; the scale → Debo or Me deben (the heavier side); savings / a pocket → Libros → Ahorros / Meta / Retiro / Emergencia; a category → its book (Fijos, Variables, Compras, Cuotas, Ahorros); the six months → Estudio; the rate → Cambio.
- Data (web, src/lib/faro/widget.ts, new): buildWidgetSnapshot() for the calendar month of today, whatever month the screen shows: summarize() (Tengo, Gastaré, Queda, Usado, Pagos, tone, savings flow and goal), netBalance() (Me deben, Debo), Central's Ahorros band (pockets + accounts, savingsOf()), categorySlices() now and last month, dated cuotas (overdue too) and credit cards with a known due date and balance (minimum payment) within 31 days, six months of summarize(). Integer minor units already in the view currency; ISO days (the app counts days left itself, so they stay right after midnight); no card digits, no people's names, no notes. Two categories with one name get their book ("Casa · fijos"); lines with no category are named by their kind (Cuotas, Compras). widget-host.tsx (mounted only inside the app): pushes the snapshot through FaroNative.setWidgetData 0.7 s after a ledger change, on open, when Faro goes to the background, and when the hide choice or the fingerprint lock changes; sample mode never feeds the widget; signing out clears it. Taps: the app keeps the target, the page takes it with FaroNative.takeOpen() after the lock and goes there like the manual's "Ir allí" (goTopic).
- Ocultar montos (decision): the widget hides amounts by default when the fingerprint lock is on for this device, and shows them when it is off; Ajustes → "Widget: ocultar montos" (only inside the Android app, the Datos de ejemplo row and switch, no new visuals) overrides it either way (device-only key faro-widget-hide). Hidden = the big number gives way to "Montos ocultos · toca para abrir", every amount is dots; bars, days and names stay; a tap opens Faro, which asks for the fingerprint. No reveal on the home screen itself, because that would bypass the lock. Manual: new topic widget-android (ES+EN).
- Phone UI of the web app: unchanged in the browser and the PWA (nothing mounts without FaroNative; na111 A2b checks the row is absent).
- Android (FaroWidgets.kt + RemoteViews layouts, android-N): PhoneWidget (4×2 default, 4×1 to 4×4) and PanelWidget (6×4 default); Android 12+ gets size-mapped layouts (the launcher swaps them on resize), Android 11 picks one from the size; previewLayout = the real layout, previewImage PNG for Android 11; corner radius = the launcher's own (system_app_widget_background_radius); day / night follow the phone (values-night: charcoal #121316 near-neutral, teal #3fd6f5, orange #ff9d2a, amber #ffc23a, purple #ba92fd; day: paper #F7F6F3). Text never wraps or ellipsizes (one line, autosize). Snapshot kept in the app's private SharedPreferences faro-widget; no network; "Abre Faro una vez" until the first snapshot. Refresh: every snapshot, a midnight tick (inexact alarm), time/zone/locale change, and every 3 h.
- Tests: tsc 5 known; npm test 59/59 + test:faro 107/107 (incl. new src/lib/faro/widget.test.ts: Central, the scale, savings, dues order, Estudio categories + last month, six months, privacy, hide rule); browser-test/na111.mjs gets E (mocked setWidgetData/takeOpen, a seeded month: snapshot = Central's figures, Ajustes row one line + switch, taps → Pagos, Fijos, Meta, − list) ALL PASS 34/34 Tron 412 and Claro 360r20 on the publish build (A–D unchanged). Phone unchanged: 8 screens (Central, Libros, Variables, Me deben, Debo, Ahorros, Estudio, Ajustes) pixel-identical (AE 0) to live 1.3.120 at Tron 412 and Claro 360r20. Android: CI build + Robolectric native-graphics shots of the real layouts (.github/workflows/widget-shots.yml, manual trigger; WidgetShotsTest also checks formatting) — phone 4×1 / 4×2 / 4×3 / hidden / empty and tablet M / L / 6×4, night and day. Not verifiable here: the real One UI launcher (cell sizes, picker preview, resize), taps landing on the device, the fingerprint flow from a widget tap. Self-review only.
- Published from a clean clone of `source` (1.3.120, 2e70d7e) + only these changes (web-121.patch; package.json test:faro merged with 1.3.117–1.3.120's tests).

## Faro para Android android-4 — bridge hardening (2026-11-06, Oscar approved 07:41)
- Shell only (android/app/.../MainActivity.kt, inner class Bridge). Every @JavascriptInterface method now answers only the trusted Faro page (`trusted` = main frame is https://oscarvelz.github.io/Faro/… or its own blob:/data:, set in onPageStarted/onPageFinished), the same way sharedCount already did. Newly checked: version → "", atRoot → no-op, canAuth → "no", saveChunk → "error", saveEnd → "error", sharedInfo → "{}", sharedClear → no-op. Already checked and unchanged: setBars, authenticate, saveBegin, sharedCount, sharedChunk, setWidgetData, takeOpen. FaroWidgets.kt exposes nothing to JS. No exception and nothing visible on an untrusted page.
- Untouched: web code, design, targetSdk 34, Gradle, versioning (versionCode = Actions run number), offline packaging.
- Code review (no device): the Faro page is trusted from onPageStarted, before any script runs, so native() feature detection, lock.ts (canAuth/authenticate → BiometricPrompt), downloads (saveBegin/Chunk/End → Download/Faro), share-inbox (sharedCount/Info/Chunk/Clear), bars (setBars), Back (FaroBack → atRoot) and widgets (setWidgetData/takeOpen) take the same path as android-3. The offline error page is loaded with base URL home, so it stays trusted as before.
- Build: source 27675ca → Actions "Faro for Android" run 4 (unsigned artifact faro-unsigned-4), signed privately (apksigner v3, alias faro), android-signed c933eec → publish run 3 → release android-4. https://github.com/Oscarvelz/Faro/releases/latest/download/Faro.apk serves it (sha256 8abe357a…6e98, versionCode 4, versionName 1.4, cert SHA-256 8f:e1:ad:7c:…:4b:98, same key → updates in place). Widget shots workflow run 7 on 27675ca: assembleRelease + testReleaseUnitTest (WidgetShotsTest) BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
- Not verified here (needs the phone): install-over-update, fingerprint, Descargas › Faro, sharing photos in, bars, Back, widgets on One UI. Self-review only.

## 1.3.123 (2026-11-06) — Faro para Android: no dark frame behind the cards after unlocking (day themes)
- Oscar (phone, android-4, 18:28): after unlocking, for a few seconds the page behind the cards stayed black/navy on a day theme (1.3.23 had fixed a similar flash at launch).
- Cause: day themes (claro, lino, costa, marfil, niebla, piedra, and the night palettes too) paint no page background (html/body transparent); the browser shows its own canvas, but inside the Android app the WebView's own background shows, which MainActivity sets to the status-bar colour (setBars → web.setBackgroundColor). While the lock gate is up that colour is the gate's navy rgb(11,24,40); after the fingerprint the books render before statusbar.ts → setBars catch up (120 + 160 ms debounces + the native round trip), so the gaps between cards stayed navy. Not a regression of 1.3.23 (that was html.booting at launch); the lock path in the app never had it covered.

Not under the sign-in/lock gate (it keeps its night sky), not during the launch splash (html.booting), Tron/Ion untouched (their body backgrounds). Browser and PWA unchanged (no data-android). No Android change, android-4 stays.

## 1.3.124 (2026-11-06) — Deslizar desde cualquier punto
- Oscar (phone, 18:41): swiping between pills/tabs only worked from some spots; it must work from anywhere (Estudio/Asesor, Libros books, Central), except things whose own sideways drag does something.
- Cause: the swipe guard in shell.tsx (since 1.3.94) skipped every pointerdown inside `svg:not(.lucide), canvas`, so a swipe that began on a chart (Estudio donut/tower, Me deben/Debo ring, book charts) was dropped; and the (?) buttons stop pointerdown propagation, so they were dead spots too. Also an early vertical wobble locked the gesture to 'y' for good.
- Fix (shell.tsx only): `swipeBlocked()` helper used by both mains, listener moved to `onPointerDownCapture` (so (?) buttons and other stopPropagation children no longer eat it); charts/svg/canvas no longer block. Still blocked (exceptions kept): input/textarea/select/contenteditable, .cal-sheet, .search-pop, .cobros (its own in/out swipe), .vault-lever-rail, colour picker (.cp-sv/.cp-hue), [role=slider], [data-noswipe], any ancestor with touch-action:none, any ancestor that is a real horizontal scroller (overflow-x auto/scroll and actually overflowing), and anything portaled outside main (sheets, bubbles). Axis 'y' can still upgrade to 'x' while the finger keeps moving.
- Tests (publish build): tsc 5 known; npm test 59 + test:faro 122 PASS; new browser-test/sw123.mjs (base 1.3.123 fails Estudio 6/6, Me deben/Debo ring 6/10): Tron 412, Claro 412, Claro 360r20 study/books/central/vert ALL PASS (Cobros keeps its own swipe, vault lever spots step the ring, vertical scroll does not change tab); tablet 1970×1200 Tron + Claro Estudio mid/chart/card + Central ring ALL PASS; tb114F ALL PASS; bk-back ALL PASS; drop95c fwd/back/taps WORST 0; nf97 Claro 14/14, Tron 11/12 (Central landing #1 fails identically on 1.3.123 = preexisting); ph114 Tron 412 + Claro 360r20 8/8 AE 0 vs 1.3.123. Not verifiable here: real One UI touch on the S24 Ultra / Tab S10. Self-review only.

## 1.3.127 (2026-11-07) — Tableta: Estudio repartido entre las dos columnas
- Oscar (Tab S10 Ultra, 00:35): in Estudio the left column ran all the way down while the right one had only three cards; the cards must be shared evenly between both halves.
- Cause: StudyStack (charts.tsx) split by section since 1.3.84: Este mes / ¿Vas bien? / Con el tiempo / Para los curiosos always left, A dónde va / Tu colchón right.
- Fix (charts.tsx StudyStack only, ≥ 1100 px one pane): cards keep the user's Estudio order (same as the phone) and each goes to the column that ends higher (greedy by measured height); a section label travels with the card under it; the right column starts level with the left one's first card. A card's column depends only on the cards before it, so a folded/unfolded card never moves. After any tap the columns only restack in place until nothing has resized for 360 ms (max 1.6 s), then cards that should change column glide there (240 ms, off with reduced motion); without a tap (first paint, data, rotation) the balance is live; heights read at column width; re-check 0.7/1.6 s after opening. Empty cards (only pin/fold buttons, e.g. Números clave with no data) and their orphan label are hidden on tablet (they floated stray buttons over the right column). Portrait 768–1099, phone landscape, split panes and phones unchanged. Asesor already uses a balanced 3-column masonry: unchanged. Cards, design and content unchanged.
- Column heights Estudio (sample, L/R px) before → after: Tron 1970×1200 4233/2892 → 3597/3556; Claro 1970×1200 4344/2932 → 3664/3640; Tron 1200×1970 4455/2637 → 3608/3737; Claro 1200×1970 4565/2903 → 3675/3821; Tron 1850×1155 4254/2869 → 3595/3556; Tron 1155×1850 4476/2854 → 3621/3737. Empty data: Tron 1970×1200 2727/1292 → 1964/2055; Claro 1200×1970 2920/1195 → 2099/2016.
